Excellent! Now I have comprehensive information. Let me create a detailed summary document:

## Semantic Release Detail Page - Frontend Architecture Report

I've completed a thorough exploration of the semantic release detail page component and the surrounding frontend architecture. Here's what I found:

---

### 1. **Page Layout & Current Fields**

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`

The detail page displays:

- **Back Navigation** - Link to `/semantic-releases` with arrow icon
- **Byline** - Project name in italic serif font (Fraunces)
- **Version Heading** - Large h1 with version number (42px, bold)
- **Meta Line** - Status with dot indicator + age (time ago) + Delete button
- **Divider** - Horizontal line
- **Error State** - Red alert box if `sr.error` present
- **Summary Section** - Large paragraph of report summary text
- **Urgency Callout** - Only shown for HIGH/CRITICAL urgency (colored alert box with icon)
- **Two-Column Cards** - Availability and Adoption sections side-by-side
- **Recommendation Blockquote** - Italicized pull-quote with left accent border (orange accent: #e8601a)
- **Source Releases Table** - Paginated table showing linked source releases (Provider, Repository, Version, Date)

---

### 2. **API Data Fetching**

**Client Library:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`

The component uses **SWR** (stale-while-revalidate) for data fetching:

```typescript
// Semantic releases API
export const semanticReleases = {
  listAll: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    request<ApiResponse<SemanticRelease[]>>(`/semantic-releases?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  list: (projectId: string, page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    request<ApiResponse<SemanticRelease[]>>(`/projects/${projectId}/semantic-releases?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
  get: (id: string) =>
    request<ApiResponse<SemanticRelease>>(`/semantic-releases/${id}`),
  delete: (id: string) =>
    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/semantic-releases/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
};
```

**Data Fetched in Detail Component:**
- `srApi.get(srId)` - Main semantic release data
- `projectsApi.get(projectId)` - Project name and metadata
- `releasesApi.listByProject(projectId)` - All source releases for that project
- `sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId)` - Source definitions (for provider badges, repo names)

---

### 3. **TypeScript Types**

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`

```typescript
export interface SemanticRelease {
  id: string;
  project_id: string;
  version: string;
  report?: SemanticReport;
  status: string;
  error?: string;
  created_at: string;
  completed_at?: string;
}

export interface SemanticReport {
  summary: string;
  availability: string;
  adoption: string;
  urgency: string;
  recommendation: string;
}
```

**Go Backend Types (richer schema):**  
`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/semantic_release.go`

```go
type SemanticReport struct {
  // SRE-focused fields
  Subject          string   `json:"subject"`
  RiskLevel        string   `json:"risk_level"`
  RiskReason       string   `json:"risk_reason"`
  StatusChecks     []string `json:"status_checks"`
  ChangelogeSummary string  `json:"changelog_summary"`
  DownloadCommands []string `json:"download_commands,omitempty"`
  DownloadLinks    []string `json:"download_links,omitempty"`
  
  // Existing fields
  Summary        string `json:"summary,omitempty"`
  Availability   string `json:"availability"`
  Adoption       string `json:"adoption"`
  Urgency        string `json:"urgency"`
  Recommendation string `json:"recommendation"`
}

type SemanticRelease struct {
  ID          string          `json:"id"`
  ProjectID   string          `json:"project_id"`
  Version     string          `json:"version"`
  Report      json.RawMessage `json:"report,omitempty"`
  Status      string          `json:"status"`
  Error       string          `json:"error,omitempty"`
  CreatedAt   time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
  CompletedAt *time.Time      `json:"completed_at,omitempty"`
}
```

---

### 4. **UI Patterns & Component Library**

**Design Stack:**
- **Framework:** Next.js 15+ with React
- **Styling:** Tailwind CSS + inline styles (mix of both approaches)
- **Component Library:** shadcn/ui (but mostly custom-built)
- **Icons:** Lucide React + react-icons (FaGithub, FaDocker for badges)
- **Fonts:** 
  - **Serif Display:** Fraunces (for headings, project names)
  - **Body:** DM Sans (for most text)
  - **Monospace:** JetBrains Mono (for version chips, IDs)

**Custom UI Components Used:**
- `StatusDot` - Colored indicator (completed/running/pending/failed)
- `VersionChip` - Monospace badge with light gray background
- `ProviderBadge` - Provider icons (GitHub, Docker) with colored backgrounds
- `UrgencyCallout` - Alert box for HIGH/CRITICAL urgency
- `SectionLabel` - Uppercase section headers in gray

**Color Palette:**
- Primary accent: `#e8601a` (orange)
- Background: `#fafaf9` (off-white)
- Surface: `#ffffff`
- Text primary: `#111113` (dark)
- Text secondary: `#6b7280`
- Text muted: `#9ca3af`
- Success: `#16a34a` (green)
- Error: `#dc2626` (red)
- Sidebar: `#16181c` (dark)

**Responsive:** Uses `max-w-[760px]` center container on detail page

---

### 5. **URL Routing**

**Detail Page Route Structure:**
```
/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/[srId]/page.tsx
```

**Key Files:**
- Server component (async): `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/projects/[id]/semantic-releases/[srId]/page.tsx`
- Client component (use client): `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`

**Page generation:**
```typescript
export async function generateStaticParams() {
  return [{ id: "0", srId: "0" }];
}
```

**Related Routes:**
- `/semantic-releases` - Global list (uses `semanticReleases.listAll()`)
- `/projects/[id]/semantic-releases` - Project-scoped list (uses `semanticReleases.list(projectId)`)
- `/projects/[id]` - Project detail page

---

### 6. **Layout & Navigation Patterns**

**Root Layout:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/app/layout.tsx`
- Two-column grid: Sidebar (52-200px collapsible) + Main content
- Sidebar contains navigation to: Dashboard, Projects, Releases, Semantic Releases, Channels, Subscriptions
- Active nav item highlighted with orange left border and subtle background
- Main content area has `p-6` padding, `overflow-y-auto`

**Typography Hierarchy:**
- Page title: 24px, Fraunces, bold
- Detail heading: 42px, Fraunces, bold
- Section labels: 11px uppercase, gray
- Body text: 14-16px, DM Sans
- Meta text: 13px, gray

---

### 7. **Related Components & Patterns**

**List View Component:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-releases-list.tsx`
- Table layout with sortable columns
- Project filter dropdown
- Pagination (Previous/Next buttons)
- Delete with confirmation dialog
- Urgency chip colors: red (#dc2626) for critical, orange (#f97316) for high

**Existing UI Components:**
- `/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` - Modal confirmation
- `/components/ui/badge.tsx`, `button.tsx`, `input.tsx` - shadcn components
- `/components/layout/sidebar.tsx` - Main navigation

---

### 8. **Key Design Observations**

1. **Data Fetching Strategy:** Component fetches independently from parent, uses SWR hooks for caching and revalidation
2. **Error Handling:** Graceful loading states, error message display in red alert
3. **Responsive Grid:** 2-column card layout (Availability/Adoption) uses `grid grid-cols-2 gap-4`
4. **Delete Flow:** Confirmation dialog before deletion, redirect to list on success
5. **Table Structure:** Source releases shown in refined table with providers, repos, versions, dates
6. **No External UI Library:** Most components are custom-built with Tailwind + inline styles (minimal shadcn usage)
7. **Accent Usage:** Single accent color (#e8601a) appears in: blockquote border, navigation active state, buttons

---

### Summary

This is a well-structured Next.js frontend with **clear separation of concerns**: data fetching via SWR, custom UI components, and a consistent design language. The detail page follows a **top-to-bottom story structure** (navigation → heading → metadata → content → related items). The backend appears richer (with SRE-focused fields like risk_level, status_checks, changelog_summary) than what the frontend currently displays, suggesting there's room for enhancement.

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"net/http"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// SemanticReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for semantic releases.
    11→type SemanticReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListAllSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)
    13→	ListSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)
    14→	GetSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.SemanticRelease, error)
    15→	GetSemanticReleaseSources(ctx context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error)
    16→	DeleteSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    17→}
    18→
    19→// SemanticReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /semantic-releases resource.
    20→type SemanticReleasesHandler struct {
    21→	store SemanticReleasesStore
    22→}
    23→
    24→// NewSemanticReleasesHandler returns a new SemanticReleasesHandler.
    25→func NewSemanticReleasesHandler(store SemanticReleasesStore) *SemanticReleasesHandler {
    26→	return &SemanticReleasesHandler{store: store}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ListAll handles GET /semantic-releases — returns all semantic releases across all projects.
    30→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) ListAll(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    31→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    32→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListAllSemanticReleases(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list semantic releases")
    35→		return
    36→	}
    37→	if releases == nil {
    38→		releases = []models.SemanticRelease{}
    39→	}
    40→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    41→}
    42→
    43→// List handles GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases — returns a paginated list of semantic releases for a project.
    44→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    45→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    46→	if projectID == "" {
    47→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    48→		return
    49→	}
    50→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    51→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListSemanticReleases(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list semantic releases")
    54→		return
    55→	}
    56→	if releases == nil {
    57→		releases = []models.SemanticRelease{}
    58→	}
    59→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    60→}
    61→
    62→// Get handles GET /semantic-releases/{id} — returns a single semantic release.
    63→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    64→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    65→	if id == "" {
    66→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    67→		return
    68→	}
    69→	sr, err := h.store.GetSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    72→		return
    73→	}
    74→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, sr)
    75→}
    76→
    77→// Delete handles DELETE /semantic-releases/{id} — deletes a single semantic release.
    78→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    79→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    80→	if id == "" {
    81→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    82→		return
    83→	}
    84→	if err := h.store.DeleteSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
    85→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    86→		return
    87→	}
    88→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
    89→}
    90→

     1→// web/lib/api/client.ts
     2→import type {
     3→  ApiResponse,
     4→  Project,
     5→  ProjectInput,
     6→  Release,
     7→  Source,
     8→  SourceInput,
     9→  Subscription,
    10→  SubscriptionInput,
    11→  NotificationChannel,
    12→  ChannelInput,
    13→  ContextSource,
    14→  ContextSourceInput,
    15→  SemanticRelease,
    16→  AgentRun,
    17→  HealthStatus,
    18→  Stats,
    19→  TrendData,
    20→} from "./types";
    21→
    22→const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || "/api/v1";
    23→
    24→async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    25→  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    26→    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...init?.headers },
    27→    ...init,
    28→  });
    29→  if (!res.ok) {
    30→    const body = await res.json().catch(() => null);
    31→    throw new Error(body?.error?.message ?? `Request failed: ${res.status}`);
    32→  }
    33→  if (res.status === 204) return null as T;
    34→  return res.json();
    35→}
    36→
    37→// --- Projects ---
    38→
    39→export const projects = {
    40→  list: (page = 1, perPage = 25) =>
    41→    request<ApiResponse<Project[]>>(`/projects?page=${page}&per_page=${perPage}`),
    42→  get: (id: string) =>
    43→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`),
    44→  create: (input: ProjectInput) =>
    45→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>("/projects", {
    46→      method: "POST",
    47→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    48→    }),
    49→  update: (id: string, input: ProjectInput) =>
    50→    request<ApiResponse<Project>>(`/projects/${id}`, {
    51→      method: "PUT",
    52→      body: JSON.stringify(input),
    53→    }),
    54→  delete: (id: string) =>
    55→    request<ApiResponse<null>>(`/projects/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" }),
    56→};
    57→
    58→// --- Sources (nested under projects) ---
    59→
    60→export const sources = {
    61→  listByProject: (projectId: string, page = 1) =>

```
     1→import { AlertTriangle, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
     2→import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
     3→
     4→interface UrgencyCalloutProps {
     5→  urgency: string;
     6→  description?: string;
     7→  className?: string;
     8→}
     9→
    10→export function UrgencyCallout({ urgency, description, className }: UrgencyCalloutProps) {
    11→  const upper = urgency?.toUpperCase();
    12→  if (upper !== "HIGH" && upper !== "CRITICAL") return null;
    13→
    14→  const isCritical = upper === "CRITICAL";
    15→  const bg = isCritical ? "#fff1f2" : "#fff8f0";
    16→  const border = isCritical ? "#dc2626" : "#d97706";
    17→  const Icon = isCritical ? AlertCircle : AlertTriangle;
    18→
    19→  return (
    20→    <div
    21→      className={cn("rounded px-4 py-3 text-sm", className)}
    22→      style={{ backgroundColor: bg, borderLeft: `3px solid ${border}` }}
    23→    >
    24→      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
    25→        <Icon className="h-4 w-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0" style={{ color: border }} />
    26→        <div>
    27→          <span className="font-semibold" style={{ color: border }}>
    28→            {upper} URGENCY
    29→          </span>
    30→          {description && (
    31→            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[#374151]">{description}</p>
    32→          )}
    33→        </div>
    34→      </div>
    35→    </div>
    36→  );
    37→}
    38→

> AGENT

Here's the proposed design for the redesigned semantic release detail page. I'll walk through each section:

## Approach: Information-Dense Briefing Layout

The page becomes an **operational intelligence briefing** rather than a generic report viewer. The layout prioritizes actionable information in a top-down hierarchy:

### Page Structure (top to bottom)

**1. Header Area** (unchanged top portion)
- Back link, project name byline, version heading (h1)
- Meta line: status dot + age + delete button

**2. Subject Line** (NEW)
- The `subject` field rendered as a prominent subtitle below the version
- Example: *"Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum (geth) v1.16.7 (Mainnet Hard Fork + Crypto Fix)"*

**3. Risk & Urgency Banner** (REDESIGNED)
- Full-width colored banner combining risk level + urgency
- Color-coded: CRITICAL = red, HIGH = orange, MEDIUM = yellow, LOW = green
- Shows risk level badge, urgency level, and `risk_reason` text
- Replaces the less prominent `UrgencyCallout`

**4. Status Checks & Downloads Row** (NEW)
- Horizontal row of status check badges (e.g., "Docker Image Verified", "Binaries Available")  
- Each check shown as a pill with checkmark icon
- Below: download links as clickable pills with external link icon
- Below: download commands as copiable code blocks (click to copy)

**5. Adoption Recommendation** (MOVED UP)
- Short adoption guidance text, only shown if present
- Rendered as a subtle callout card

**6. Related Source Releases** (FIXED)
- Fetched from NEW endpoint `GET /semantic-releases/{id}/sources` (only linked releases)
- Compact table with provider badge + repository + version chip (clickable link to release URL)
- Version is clickable — links to the source release (GitHub release page / Docker Hub tag)

**7. Context Sources** (NEW)
- Fetched from existing `GET /projects/{projectId}/context-sources`
- List of reference links the agent used (runbooks, docs, etc.)
- Shown as clickable link cards with name and type badge

**8. Changelog Summary** (RENAMED from "Summary")
- The `changelog_summary` field — the meaty technical breakdown
- Rendered as prose text

**9. Recommendation** (KEPT)
- Pull-quote blockquote with accent border — already works well

### Backend Changes
- Add `GET /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}/sources` endpoint
- Wire to existing `GetSemanticReleaseSources` store method

### Frontend Changes
- Update `SemanticReport` TypeScript interface with all 12 backend fields
- Rewrite `semantic-release-detail.tsx` with the new layout
- Add new API client method `semanticReleases.getSources(id)`
- Fetch context sources for the project
- Build release source URLs from provider + repository + version

Does this layout and information hierarchy look right? Anything you'd change about the ordering or what to include/exclude?

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"net/http"
     6→
     7→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// SemanticReleasesStore defines the persistence operations for semantic releases.
    11→type SemanticReleasesStore interface {
    12→	ListAllSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)
    13→	ListSemanticReleases(ctx context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error)
    14→	GetSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) (*models.SemanticRelease, error)
    15→	GetSemanticReleaseSources(ctx context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error)
    16→	DeleteSemanticRelease(ctx context.Context, id string) error
    17→}
    18→
    19→// SemanticReleasesHandler implements HTTP handlers for the /semantic-releases resource.
    20→type SemanticReleasesHandler struct {
    21→	store SemanticReleasesStore
    22→}
    23→
    24→// NewSemanticReleasesHandler returns a new SemanticReleasesHandler.
    25→func NewSemanticReleasesHandler(store SemanticReleasesStore) *SemanticReleasesHandler {
    26→	return &SemanticReleasesHandler{store: store}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// ListAll handles GET /semantic-releases — returns all semantic releases across all projects.
    30→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) ListAll(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    31→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    32→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListAllSemanticReleases(r.Context(), page, perPage)
    33→	if err != nil {
    34→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list semantic releases")
    35→		return
    36→	}
    37→	if releases == nil {
    38→		releases = []models.SemanticRelease{}
    39→	}
    40→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    41→}
    42→
    43→// List handles GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases — returns a paginated list of semantic releases for a project.
    44→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) List(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    45→	projectID := r.PathValue("projectId")
    46→	if projectID == "" {
    47→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Project ID is required")
    48→		return
    49→	}
    50→	page, perPage := ParsePagination(r)
    51→	releases, total, err := h.store.ListSemanticReleases(r.Context(), projectID, page, perPage)
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusInternalServerError, "internal_error", "Failed to list semantic releases")
    54→		return
    55→	}
    56→	if releases == nil {
    57→		releases = []models.SemanticRelease{}
    58→	}
    59→	RespondList(w, r, http.StatusOK, releases, page, perPage, total)
    60→}
    61→
    62→// Get handles GET /semantic-releases/{id} — returns a single semantic release.
    63→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Get(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    64→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    65→	if id == "" {
    66→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    67→		return
    68→	}
    69→	sr, err := h.store.GetSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    72→		return
    73→	}
    74→	RespondJSON(w, r, http.StatusOK, sr)
    75→}
    76→
    77→// Delete handles DELETE /semantic-releases/{id} — deletes a single semantic release.
    78→func (h *SemanticReleasesHandler) Delete(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    79→	id := r.PathValue("id")
    80→	if id == "" {
    81→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_request", "Semantic release ID is required")
    82→		return
    83→	}
    84→	if err := h.store.DeleteSemanticRelease(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
    85→		RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
    86→		return
    87→	}
    88→	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
    89→}

    15→// mockSemanticReleasesStore implements SemanticReleasesStore for testing.
    16→type mockSemanticReleasesStore struct {
    17→	releases   []models.SemanticRelease
    18→	sources    []models.Release
    19→	listErr    error
    20→	getErr     error
    21→	sourcesErr error
    22→	deleteErr  error

    24→
    25→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) ListAllSemanticReleases(_ context.Context, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error) {
    26→	if m.listErr != nil {
    27→		return nil, 0, m.listErr
    28→	}
    29→	return m.releases, len(m.releases), nil
    30→}
    31→
    32→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) ListSemanticReleases(_ context.Context, projectID string, page, perPage int) ([]models.SemanticRelease, int, error) {
    33→	if m.listErr != nil {
    34→		return nil, 0, m.listErr
    35→	}
    36→	return m.releases, len(m.releases), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) GetSemanticRelease(_ context.Context, id string) (*models.SemanticRelease, error) {
    40→	if m.getErr != nil {
    41→		return nil, m.getErr
    42→	}
    43→	for i := range m.releases {
    44→		if m.releases[i].ID == id {
    45→			return &m.releases[i], nil
    46→		}
    47→	}
    48→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("not found")
    49→}
    50→
    51→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) GetSemanticReleaseSources(_ context.Context, id string) ([]models.Release, error) {
    52→	if m.sourcesErr != nil {
    53→		return nil, m.sourcesErr
    54→	}
    55→	return m.sources, nil
    56→}
    57→
    58→func (m *mockSemanticReleasesStore) DeleteSemanticRelease(_ context.Context, id string) error {
    59→	if m.deleteErr != nil {
    60→		return m.deleteErr
    61→	}
    62→	for i := range m.releases {
    63→		if m.releases[i].ID == id {
    64→			m.releases = append(m.releases[:i], m.releases[i+1:]...)
    65→			return nil
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return fmt.Errorf("not found")
    69→}
    70→
    71→func setupSemanticReleasesMux(store SemanticReleasesStore) *http.ServeMux {
    72→	h := NewSemanticReleasesHandler(store)
    73→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
    74→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /semantic-releases", h.ListAll)
    75→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases", h.List)
    76→	mux.HandleFunc("GET /semantic-releases/{id}", h.Get)
    77→	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /semantic-releases/{id}", h.Delete)
    78→	return mux
    79→}

     1→package api
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"net/http"
     5→
     6→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
     7→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/ingestion"
     8→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/routing"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// Dependencies holds all external dependencies required for registering API routes.
    12→type Dependencies struct {
    13→	DB                    *pgxpool.Pool
    14→	ProjectsStore         ProjectsStore
    15→	ReleasesStore         ReleasesStore
    16→	SubscriptionsStore    SubscriptionsStore
    17→	SourcesStore          SourcesStore
    18→	ChannelsStore         ChannelsStore
    19→	ContextSourcesStore   ContextSourcesStore
    20→	SemanticReleasesStore SemanticReleasesStore
    21→	AgentStore            AgentStore
    22→	KeyStore              KeyStore
    23→	HealthChecker         HealthChecker
    24→	Broadcaster           *Broadcaster
    25→	NoAuth                bool
    26→	IngestionService      *ingestion.Service
    27→	HTTPClient            *http.Client
    28→}
    29→
    30→// RegisterRoutes registers all API v1 routes on the given ServeMux.
    31→func RegisterRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, deps Dependencies) {
    32→	// Public chain: no auth required.
    33→	publicChain := Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery)
    34→
    35→	// Authenticated chain: includes auth unless NoAuth is set.
    36→	chain := publicChain
    37→	if !deps.NoAuth {
    38→		chain = Chain(RequestID, Logger, Recovery, RateLimit(10, 20), Auth(deps.KeyStore))
    39→	}
    40→
    41→	// Projects (CRUD)
    42→	projects := NewProjectsHandler(deps.ProjectsStore)
    43→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.List)))
    44→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Create)))
    45→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Get)))
    46→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Update)))
    47→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/projects/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(projects.Delete)))
    48→
    49→	// Sources (nested under projects)
    50→	sources := NewSourcesHandler(deps.SourcesStore, deps.IngestionService, deps.HTTPClient)
    51→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.List)))
    52→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Create)))
    53→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Get)))
    54→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Update)))
    55→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.Delete)))
    56→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/sources/{id}/poll", chain(http.HandlerFunc(sources.FetchReleases)))
    57→
    58→	// Context Sources (nested under projects)
    59→	contextSources := NewContextSourcesHandler(deps.ContextSourcesStore)
    60→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.List)))
    61→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/context-sources", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Create)))
    62→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Get)))
    63→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Update)))
    64→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/context-sources/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(contextSources.Delete)))
    65→
    66→	// Releases (read-only, nested under sources and projects)
    67→	releases := NewReleasesHandler(deps.ReleasesStore)
    68→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.List)))
    69→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/sources/{id}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListBySource)))
    70→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.ListByProject)))
    71→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(releases.Get)))
    72→
    73→	// Semantic Releases (nested under projects + top-level list)
    74→	semanticReleases := NewSemanticReleasesHandler(deps.SemanticReleasesStore)
    75→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/semantic-releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.ListAll)))
    76→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.List)))
    77→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.Get)))
    78→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.Delete)))
    79→
    80→	// Subscriptions (CRUD)
    81→	subscriptions := NewSubscriptionsHandler(deps.SubscriptionsStore)
    82→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.List)))
    83→	mux.Handle("POST /api/v1/subscriptions", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Create)))
    84→	mux.Handle("GET /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Get)))
    85→	mux.Handle("PUT /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Update)))
    86→	mux.Handle("DELETE /api/v1/subscriptions/{id}", chain(http.HandlerFunc(subscriptions.Delete)))
    87→

   237→   - **Version heading** — h1 at 42px
   238→   - **Subject** — `sr.report.subject` rendered as a subtitle (20px, DM Sans, `text-[#374151]`)

   241→   - **Error state** (if applicable)

Color-coded by `risk_level`: CRITICAL=`#dc2626`/`#fff1f2`, HIGH=`#d97706`/`#fff8f0`, MEDIUM=`#ca8a04`/`#fefce8`, LOW=`#16a34a`/`#f0fdf4`. Shows risk level as uppercase badge, urgency level, and `risk_reason` as body text. Replaces the old `<UrgencyCallout>`.

Version chips are clickable — for GitHub sources link to `https://github.com/{repository}/releases/tag/{version}`, for Docker sources link to `https://hub.docker.com/r/{repository}/tags?name={version}`.
   249→   - **Context Sources** — List of context source cards from `contextSources.list(projectId)`. Each shows name, type badge, and a link extracted from `config.url` if present. Only shown if context sources exist.

Register the route in `internal/api/server.go`\n\nAfter the DELETE route for semantic-releases (line 78), add:\n\n```go\nmux.Handle(\"GET /api/v1/semantic-releases/{id}/sources\", chain(http.HandlerFunc(semanticReleases.ListSources)))\n```\n\n### 3. Update test setup in `internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go`\n\nUpdate `setupSemanticReleasesMux` to include the new route:\n\n```go\nfunc setupSemanticReleasesMux(store SemanticReleasesStore) *http.ServeMux {\n\th := NewSemanticReleasesHandler(store)\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /semantic-releases\", h.ListAll)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /projects/{projectId}/semantic-releases\", h.List)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /semantic-releases/{id}\", h.Get)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /semantic-releases/{id}/sources\", h.ListSources)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"DELETE /semantic-releases/{id}\", h.Delete)\n\treturn mux\n}\n```\n\n### 4. Add two tests\n\n```go\nfunc TestSemanticReleasesHandlerListSources(t *testing.T) {\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstore := &mockSemanticReleasesStore{\n\t\treleases: []models.SemanticRelease{\n\t\t\t{ID: \"sr-1\", ProjectID: \"p1\", Version: \"1.0.0\", Status: \"completed\", CreatedAt: now},\n\t\t},\n\t\tsources: []models.Release{\n\t\t\t{ID: \"r-1\", SourceID: \"s-1\", Version: \"v1.16.7\", CreatedAt: now},\n\t\t\t{ID: \"r-2\", SourceID: \"s-2\", Version: \"v1.16.7\", CreatedAt: now},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\tmux := setupSemanticReleasesMux(store)\n\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, \"/semantic-releases/sr-1/sources\", nil)\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusOK {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 200, got %d\", w.Code)\n\t}\n\n\tvar got struct {\n\t\tData []models.Release `json:\"data\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(w.Body).Decode(&got); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"decode: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(got.Data) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 2 source releases, got %d\", len(got.Data))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestSemanticReleasesHandlerListSourcesEmpty(t *testing.T) {\n\tstore := &mockSemanticReleasesStore{\n\t\treleases: []models.SemanticRelease{\n\t\t\t{ID: \"sr-1\", ProjectID: \"p1\", Version: \"1.0.0\", Status: \"completed\", CreatedAt: time.Now()},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\tmux := setupSemanticReleasesMux(store)\n\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, \"/semantic-releases/sr-1/sources\", nil)\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\n\tif w.Code != http.StatusOK {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected status 200, got %d\", w.Code)\n\t}\n\n\tvar raw map[string]json.RawMessage\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(w.Body).Decode(&raw); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"decode: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif string(raw[\"data\"]) != \"[]\" {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected data to be empty array [], got %s\", string(raw[\"data\"]))\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n### 5. - `TestSemanticReleasesHandlerListSources` -- verifies the endpoint returns 2 source releases when the mock store has data.

Both tasks are complete. Here is what was done:

### Task 2: Updated `SemanticReport` interface

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`

The `SemanticReport` interface was expanded from 5 required fields to 12 optional fields to match the Go backend:

```typescript
export interface SemanticReport {
  subject?: string;
  risk_level?: string;
  risk_reason?: string;
  status_checks?: string[];
  changelog_summary?: string;
  download_commands?: string[];
  download_links?: string[];
  summary?: string;
  availability?: string;
  adoption?: string;
  urgency?: string;
  recommendation?: string;
}
```

All fields are optional (`?`) to maintain backward compatibility with older reports that may not have every field.

**Commit:** `5d84eb6 feat(web): add missing SemanticReport fields to TypeScript types`

### Task 3: Added `getSources` API client method

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`

Added the `getSources` method to the `semanticReleases` object, which calls `GET /semantic-releases/{id}/sources` and returns `ApiResponse<Release[]>`:

```typescript
getSources: (id: string) =>
  request<ApiResponse<Release[]>>(`/semantic-releases/${id}/sources`),
```

**Commit:** `eaf3435 feat(web): add getSources API client method for semantic releases`

    15→export function SemanticReleaseDetail({ projectId, srId }: { projectId: string; srId: string }) {
    16→  const router = useRouter();
    17→  const { data, isLoading } = useSWR(`sr-${srId}`, () => srApi.get(srId));
    18→  const { data: projectData } = useSWR(`project-${projectId}`, () => projectsApi.get(projectId));
    19→  const { data: releasesData } = useSWR(`project-releases-${projectId}`, () => releasesApi.listByProject(projectId));
    20→  const { data: sourcesData } = useSWR(`project-sources-${projectId}`, () => sourcesApi.listByProject(projectId));
    21→
    22→  if (isLoading) {
    23→    return (
    24→      <div className="flex justify-center py-20">
    25→        <div className="text-[13px] text-[#9ca3af]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
    26→          Loading...
    27→        </div>
    28→      </div>
    29→    );
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  const sr = data?.data;
    33→  if (!sr) {
    34→    return (
    35→      <div className="flex justify-center py-20">
    36→        <div className="text-[13px] text-[#9ca3af]" style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}>
    37→          Semantic release not found
    38→        </div>
    39→      </div>
    40→    );
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const project = projectData?.data;
    44→  const releasesList = releasesData?.data ?? [];
    45→  const sourcesList = sourcesData?.data ?? [];
    46→  const sourcesById = Object.fromEntries(sourcesList.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
    47→
    48→  const handleDelete = async () => {
    49→    if (!confirm("Delete this semantic release?")) return;
    50→    await srApi.delete(srId);
    51→    router.push("/semantic-releases");
    52→  };
    53→
    54→  return (
    55→    <div className="fade-in mx-auto max-w-[760px]">
    56→      {/* Back link */}
    57→      <Link
    58→        href="/semantic-releases"
    59→        className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors hover:opacity-70"
    60→        style={{
    61→          fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)",
    62→          fontSize: "13px",
    63→          color: "#6b7280",
    64→        }}
    65→      >
    66→        <ArrowLeft size={14} />
    67→        Back to Semantic Releases
    68→      </Link>
    69→
    70→      {/* 2. Byline */}
    71→      {project?.name && (
    72→        <p
    73→          className="mb-1 text-[13px] italic text-[#9ca3af]"
    74→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    75→        >
    76→          {project.name}
    77→        </p>
    78→      )}
    79→
    80→      {/* 3. Version heading */}
    81→      <h1
    82→        className="text-[42px] font-bold tracking-tight text-[#111113] leading-[1.1]"
    83→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
    84→      >
    85→        {sr.version}
    86→      </h1>
    87→
    88→      {/* 4. Meta line */}
    89→      <div
    90→        className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]"
    91→        style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
    92→      >
    93→        <StatusDot status={sr.status} />
    94→        <span className="flex-1">
    95→          {sr.status}
    96→          {sr.completed_at && ` \u00b7 generated ${timeAgo(sr.completed_at)}`}
    97→        </span>
    98→        <button
    99→          onClick={handleDelete}
   100→          className="rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-[#991b1b] transition-colors hover:bg-[#fef2f2]"
   101→          style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   102→        >
   103→          Delete
   104→        </button>
   105→      </div>
   106→
   107→      {/* 5. Divider */}
   108→      <hr className="my-8 border-0" style={{ borderTop: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }} />
   109→
   110→      {/* 6. Error state */}
   111→      {sr.error && (
   112→        <div

   114→          style={{

   148→                className="text-[14px] leading-[1.6] text-[#111113]"
   149→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}
   150→              >
   151→                {sr.report.availability}
   152→              </p>

   159→              <p

   161→                style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-dm-sans)" }}

   164→              </p>

   167→
   168→          {/* 10. Recommendation pull-quote */}
   169→          {sr.report.recommendation && (

     1→import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
     2→import { FaGithub, FaDocker } from "react-icons/fa";
     3→import type { IconType } from "react-icons";
     4→
     5→const PROVIDER_STYLES: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; label: string; icon: IconType }> = {
     6→  github: { bg: "#1a1a1a", text: "#ffffff", label: "GitHub", icon: FaGithub },
     7→  dockerhub: { bg: "#2496ed", text: "#ffffff", label: "Docker Hub", icon: FaDocker },
     8→};
     9→
    10→export function getProviderIcon(provider: string): IconType | undefined {
    11→  return PROVIDER_STYLES[provider.toLowerCase()]?.icon;
    12→}
    13→
    14→interface ProviderBadgeProps {
    15→  provider: string;
    16→  className?: string;
    17→}
    18→
    19→export function ProviderBadge({ provider, className }: ProviderBadgeProps) {
    20→  const style = PROVIDER_STYLES[provider.toLowerCase()];
    21→  const resolved = style ?? {
    22→    bg: "#6b7280",
    23→    text: "#ffffff",
    24→    label: provider,
    25→  };
    26→  const Icon = style?.icon;
    27→  return (
    28→    <span
    29→      className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium leading-none", className)}
    30→      style={{ backgroundColor: resolved.bg, color: resolved.text }}
    31→    >
    32→      {Icon && <Icon size={11} />}

     1→export function timeAgo(dateStr?: string | null): string {
     2→  if (!dateStr) return "\u2014";
     3→  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime();
     4→  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
     5→  if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`;
     6→  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60);
     7→  if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`;
     8→  return `${Math.floor(hrs / 24)}d ago`;
     9→}
    10→
    11→export function validateRepository(provider: string, repo: string): string | null {
    12→  const trimmed = repo.trim();
    13→  if (!trimmed) return null;
    14→  if (provider === "github" && /^(https?:\/\/)?github\.com\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    15→    return "Use owner/repo format (e.g. ethereum/go-ethereum), not a full URL";
    16→  }
    17→  if (provider === "dockerhub" && /^(https?:\/\/)?hub\.docker\.com\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    18→    return "Use owner/image format (e.g. library/nginx), not a full URL";
    19→  }
    20→  if (/^https?:\/\//.test(trimmed)) {
    21→    return "Use owner/repo format, not a full URL";
    22→  }
    23→  return null;
    24→}
    25→
    26→export function formatInterval(seconds: number): string {
    27→  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
    28→  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.round(seconds / 60)}m`;
    29→  if (seconds < 86400) return `${(seconds / 3600).toFixed(1)}h`;
    30→  return `${(seconds / 86400).toFixed(1)}d`;
    31→}
    32→

The semantic release detail component has been fully rewritten and the build passes. Here is a summary of all changes made to `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`:

### Imports changed
- **Removed**: `releases as releasesApi` from the API client, `UrgencyCallout` component
- **Added**: `contextSources` from the API client, `Check`, `ExternalLink`, `Copy`, `ShieldAlert`, `BookOpen` icons from `lucide-react`

### Data fetching changed
- **Removed**: `useSWR` call to `releasesApi.listByProject(projectId)` for all project releases
- **Added**: `useSWR('sr-sources-${srId}', () => srApi.getSources(srId))` -- fetches only the source releases linked to this semantic release
- **Added**: `useSWR('project-context-sources-${projectId}', () => contextSources.list(projectId))` -- fetches context sources for the project

### New helper functions
- `getRiskColors(riskLevel)` -- maps CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW to border, background, and text colors
- `safeHostname(url)` -- extracts hostname from URL with try-catch for malformed URLs

### New sections (in order)
1. **Subject line** (section 4) -- renders `sr.report.subject` at 20px DM Sans below the version heading
2. **Risk & Urgency Banner** (section 8) -- replaces the old `UrgencyCallout` with a color-coded banner showing risk level badge, urgency text, and risk reason
3. **Availability & Downloads** (section 9) -- three sub-sections:
   - Status checks as green pills with check icons
   - Download links as clickable pills showing extracted hostnames
   - Download commands as copiable code blocks with copy-on-hover buttons
4. **Adoption** (section 10) -- conditional card showing adoption info
5. **Source Releases** (section 11) -- same table but now with clickable version chips linking to GitHub releases or Docker Hub tags
6. **Context Sources** (section 12) -- lists context sources with BookOpen icons, type badges, and external link buttons
7. **Changelog Summary** (section 13) -- renamed section rendering `changelog_summary`

### Preserved
- `fade-in` class on container
- `max-w-[760px]` container width
- Font patterns (Fraunces display, DM Sans body, JetBrains Mono code)
- `space-y-10` wrapper for all report sections
- `"use client"` directive
- Back link, project byline, version heading, meta line, divider, error state, and recommendation blockquote

---

# Code Quality Review: Semantic Release Detail Rewrite

## Overall Assessment

This is a well-structured, clean implementation. The component reads clearly from top to bottom, the section numbering in comments aids navigation, the UI primitives (`StatusDot`, `VersionChip`, `SectionLabel`, `ProviderBadge`) are appropriately reused, and the styling approach (CSS variables for fonts, Tailwind for layout, hex colors for design tokens) matches the established codebase pattern. The backend handler and tests are solid. Below are the specific findings.

---

## Strengths

- **Clean component structure**: The 14-section numbered layout in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx` is easy to navigate and mirrors the spec.
- **Good UI primitive reuse**: `StatusDot`, `VersionChip`, `SectionLabel`, and `ProviderBadge` are all shared components, avoiding duplication.
- **Defensive `safeHostname` helper** (line 40-46): Gracefully handles malformed URLs with a try/catch.
- **Null coalescing throughout**: Arrays default to `[]` via `?? []` on lines 99-102, preventing crashes on missing data.
- **Backend handler consistency**: `ListSources` at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases.go` follows the exact same pattern as `Get` and `Delete` (input validation, error wrapping, nil-to-empty-slice normalization).
- **Thorough test coverage**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go` includes happy path, empty array, error, not-found, and cross-project scenarios for `ListSources`.
- **Consistent type definitions**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts` has all fields marked with proper optionality.

---

## Issues

### Important (should fix)

**1. Delete has no error handling -- silent failure on network error**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, lines 104-108.

```typescript
const handleDelete = async () => {
  if (!confirm("Delete this semantic release?")) return;
  await srApi.delete(srId);
  router.push("/semantic-releases");
};
```

If `srApi.delete(srId)` throws (network error, 404, 500), the promise rejection is unhandled. The user sees nothing, and `router.push` never fires. Meanwhile the `request()` helper in `client.ts` (line 31) throws an `Error` on non-2xx responses, so this is a real path.

Compare with the existing `ConfirmDialog` pattern in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` which wraps the async operation in try/finally. At minimum, wrap in try/catch and show an alert or toast on failure.

**2. `confirm()` used instead of the project's `ConfirmDialog` component**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, line 105.

The codebase has a purpose-built `ConfirmDialog` component at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` with loading states and proper accessibility (dialog role, focus management). Using `window.confirm()` is inconsistent with the project's UI patterns and is inaccessible to screen readers in a controlled way.

**3. Docker Hub version not URL-encoded in source releases table**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, lines 413-414.

```typescript
? `https://hub.docker.com/r/${source.repository}/tags?name=${rel.version}`
```

The existing `release-detail.tsx` (line 31) correctly uses `encodeURIComponent(version)` for the Docker Hub URL:

```go
return `https://hub.docker.com/r/${repository}/tags?name=${encodeURIComponent(version)}`;
```

Versions like `1.0+build.1` or tags with special characters will produce broken URLs without encoding.

**4. `ListSources` handler conflates "not found" with all errors**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases.go`, lines 84-86.

```go
releases, err := h.store.GetSemanticReleaseSources(r.Context(), id)
if err != nil {
    RespondError(w, r, http.StatusNotFound, "not_found", "Semantic release not found")
```

Any store error (database timeout, connection failure) returns 404. This is the same pattern used by `Get` and `Delete` in this file, so it is locally consistent, but it masks operational errors. A database failure should return 500, not 404. This applies to the `Get` handler as well (line 70-72) but was pre-existing.

---

### Minor (nice to have)

**5. Five parallel SWR fetches on mount -- no conditional fetching for secondary data**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, lines 56-69.

All five fetches (`sr`, `project`, `srSources`, `sources`, `contextSources`) fire immediately on mount. The `sourcesData` and `contextSourcesData` are only consumed inside the report sections, and `srSourcesData` is only used if the report exists. None of these are conditioned on `data` being available first.

This is not a bug -- SWR gracefully renders with `undefined`/`[]` -- but it means unnecessary network requests when the primary resource is still loading or returns 404. The `release-detail.tsx` component (line 61-84) demonstrates the conditional SWR key pattern (`release ? \`source-${id}\` : null`) that avoids this.

**6. `sourcesById` map rebuilt on every render**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, line 101.

```typescript
const sourcesById = Object.fromEntries(sourcesList.map((s) => [s.id, s]));
```

This runs on every render. Wrapping it in `useMemo` with `[sourcesList]` as the dependency would prevent unnecessary object allocation, though in practice with small lists this is negligible.

**7. `navigator.clipboard.writeText()` may throw on insecure contexts**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, line 331.

```typescript
onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(cmd)}
```

The Clipboard API requires a secure context (HTTPS or localhost). On HTTP deployments, this call throws. A try/catch with a fallback or silent handling would be more robust.

**8. Table `last:border-b-0` Tailwind class conflicts with inline style**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, line 421.

```tsx
<tr key={rel.id} style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid #e8e8e5" }} className="last:border-b-0">
```

The inline `style` always wins over Tailwind's `last:border-b-0` class due to CSS specificity. The last row will still show a bottom border. Either use Tailwind for both (`border-b border-[#e8e8e5] last:border-b-0`) or conditionally apply the inline style.

**9. `ListSources` test does not verify the semantic release ID is validated against the store**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go`, lines 290-320.

The `GetSemanticReleaseSources` mock returns `m.sources` regardless of the `id` parameter. There is no test that calls `/semantic-releases/nonexistent/sources` when the store *does not* have that SR. The `TestSemanticReleasesHandlerListSourcesEmpty` test (line 322-345) covers the empty-sources case but still uses a valid SR ID. A test for a truly nonexistent ID returning an error path would increase coverage of the handler.

---

### Nitpick

**10. `&amp;` HTML entity in JSX is unnecessary**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, line 264.

```tsx
<SectionLabel className="mb-3">Availability &amp; Downloads</SectionLabel>
```

JSX children are not HTML -- you can write `Availability & Downloads` directly. `&amp;` works but is non-idiomatic in JSX.

**11. `getRiskColors` default case and `LOW` share a branch**

File: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`, lines 34-37.

```typescript
case "LOW":
default:
  return { border: "#16a34a", bg: "#f0fdf4", text: "#166534" };
```

This means an undefined/unknown risk level renders with green "LOW" styling. This is a design choice but could confuse users if a new risk level is added and falls through to green. Separating `LOW` from `default` with a neutral gray for unknown values would be safer.

**12. `SectionLabel` renders as `<p>` -- semantically it should be a heading**

The `SectionLabel` component at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/ui/section-label.tsx` renders a `<p>` tag. When used as a section header (e.g., "Source Releases", "Context Sources"), an `<h2>` or `<h3>` would be more semantically correct for accessibility and screen reader navigation. This is a pre-existing issue, not introduced by this rewrite.

---

## Backend Summary

The Go handler and tests are clean and well-structured:

- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases.go`: The `ListSources` handler (lines 78-93) follows the established pattern. The nil-to-empty-slice normalization (line 89-91) ensures JSON output is `[]` not `null`, matching the frontend expectation.
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/api/semantic_releases_test.go`: Good coverage with 8 distinct test functions. The `setupSemanticReleasesMux` helper (line 71) correctly registers the new route.
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/client.ts`: The `getSources` method (line 126-127) is a clean one-liner that follows the exact pattern of other resource methods.
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`: The `SemanticReport` interface (lines 106-119) has all fields properly optional, which is correct since the agent may produce partial reports.

## Verdict

The implementation is solid and ready for use after addressing the **Important** items. The two highest-priority fixes are: (1) adding error handling to `handleDelete`, and (2) URL-encoding the Docker Hub version parameter. The `confirm()` to `ConfirmDialog` migration is a consistency improvement that could be deferred if needed.

	DownloadLinks    []string `json:"download_links,omitempty"`
	// ... other fields ...
}
```

**Frontend (TypeScript)** — `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/api/types.ts`:
```typescript
export interface SemanticReport {
  // ... other fields ...
  download_links?: string[];
  // ... other fields ...
}
```

### 2. **How It's Populated: Agent Prompt**

The `download_links` field is **generated entirely by the LLM agent** (ADK-Go). It's specified in the default agent instruction in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/orchestrator.go` (line 71):

```json
{
  "download_commands": ["docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.10.15"],
  "download_links": ["https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.10.15"]
}
```

The agent receives instructions to analyze releases and produce this field, with no explicit database or platform-specific logic. The agent is given access to:

- **Release data** via the `get_releases` tool (paginated, includes raw_data)
- **Release details** via the `get_release_detail` tool (full raw_data payload)
- **Context sources** via the `list_context_sources` tool (runbooks, docs)
- **Web search** for additional context (on Gemini)

### 3. **Available Data: Raw Release Data**

The agent has access to `raw_data` from releases, which comes from upstream registries:

**GitHub Releases** (via Atom feed) — `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/github_atom.go`:
- `release_url` (metadata from Atom link)
- `changelog` (from Atom entry content)

**Docker Hub** (via API) — `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/dockerhub.go`:
- Tag names and timestamps only
- No binary/asset data included

The `raw_data` is constructed in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/ingestion/pgstore.go` (line 34-42):
```go
// Build raw_data from Metadata + Changelog so nothing is lost.
raw := make(map[string]string)
for k, v := range result.Metadata {
    raw[k] = v
}
if result.Changelog != "" {
    raw["changelog"] = result.Changelog
}
```

### 4. **Current Behavior**

- **No structured platform detection** — The code doesn't detect or enumerate platform-specific binaries (amd64, arm64, windows, etc.)
- **No asset extraction** — GitHub release assets/binaries are not explicitly fetched or exposed
- **Agent responsibilities** — The LLM agent decides what to include in `download_links` based on whatever data is available in release details
- **Example output** — Test case shows: `["https://example.com/release"]` or `["https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.10.15"]`

### 5. **Data Flow**

```
Ingestion (GitHub/DockerHub) 
  → IngestionResult { RawVersion, Changelog, Metadata }
  → raw_data (JSON) stored in database
  → Agent retrieves via get_release_detail tool
  → Agent generates download_links based on raw_data
  → SemanticReport JSON stored
  → Frontend displays as pill links
```

### 6. **Frontend Display**

From `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx` (lines 292-314):
- Links are rendered as clickable pills with hostname extracted via `safeHostname()`
- Each link opens in a new browser tab
- No special handling for platform variants or binary files

### Key Insight

**`download_links` today are completely free-form strings generated by the LLM agent.** There's no structured concept of:
- Platform-specific binaries (Windows, macOS, Linux architectures)
- Multi-file assets within a release
- Asset metadata or checksums
- Download categorization

The agent sees whatever raw data the source provides and decides what constitutes a "download link."

    74→// BuildAgent creates the ADK-Go LLM agent for a given project. This is used
    75→// by both the production orchestrator and the dev entrypoint to ensure the
    76→// same agent configuration is used everywhere.
    77→func BuildAgent(ctx context.Context, store AgentDataStore, project *models.Project, llmConfig LLMConfig, version string) (agent.Agent, error) {
    78→	instruction := DefaultInstruction
    79→	if project.AgentPrompt != "" {
    80→		instruction = project.AgentPrompt + "\n\n" + instruction
    81→	}
    82→	// Substitute version placeholder
    83→	instruction = strings.ReplaceAll(instruction, "{{VERSION}}", version)
    84→
    85→	llmModel, err := NewLLMModel(ctx, llmConfig)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create LLM model: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	// Create project-scoped function tools.
    91→	functionTools, err := NewTools(store, project.ID)
    92→	if err != nil {
    93→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create agent tools: %w", err)
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// For Gemini: use sub-agent pattern (required because Gemini can't mix
    97→	// grounding tools like GoogleSearch with function tools on the same agent).
    98→	// For OpenAI: flat architecture — give function tools directly to root agent
    99→	// (OpenAI doesn't support the agenttool schema).
   100→	if llmConfig.Provider == "openai" {
   101→		return llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   102→			Name:        "release_analyst",
   103→			Description: "Analyzes upstream releases and produces semantic release reports.",
   104→			Model:       llmModel,
   105→			Instruction: instruction,
   106→			Tools:       functionTools,
   107→		})
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	// Gemini path: sub-agent architecture.
   111→
   112→	// Data sub-agent: handles DB queries for releases and context sources.
   113→	dataAgent, err := llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   114→		Name:        "data_agent",
   115→		Description: "Query project releases and context sources from the database. Use this to fetch release lists, release details, and context sources like runbooks and documentation.",
   116→		Model:       llmModel,
   117→		Tools:       functionTools,
   118→	})
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create data sub-agent: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	// Search sub-agent: Google Search grounding.
   124→	searchAgent, err := llmagent.New(llmagent.Config{
   125→		Name:        "search_agent",
   126→		Description: "Search the web for additional context about a release. Use this ONLY when you need information not available from the project's sources, such as community sentiment, security advisories, network adoption statistics, or known issues.",
   127→		Model:       llmModel,
   128→		Tools:       []tool.Tool{geminitool.GoogleSearch{}},
   129→	})

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx",
  "old_string": "function safeHostname(url: string): string {\n  try {\n    return new URL(url).hostname.replace(\"www.\", \"\");\n  } catch {\n    return url;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "function safeHostname(url: string): string {\n  try {\n    return new URL(url).hostname.replace(\"www.\", \"\");\n  } catch {\n    return url;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction getDownloadLabel(url: string): { label: string; isDirect: boolean } {\n  const lower = url.toLowerCase();\n  const filename = lower.split(\"/\").pop() ?? \"\";\n\n  // Detect platform from common patterns in the URL or filename\n  const platforms: [RegExp, string][] = [\n    [/linux.*amd64|amd64.*linux/, \"Linux x64\"],\n    [/linux.*arm64|arm64.*linux|linux.*aarch64/, \"Linux ARM64\"],\n    [/linux.*386|linux.*i386/, \"Linux x86\"],\n    [/darwin.*arm64|arm64.*darwin|macos.*arm64|osx.*arm64/, \"macOS ARM64\"],\n    [/darwin.*amd64|amd64.*darwin|macos.*amd64|osx.*amd64/, \"macOS x64\"],\n    [/darwin|macos|osx/, \"macOS\"],\n    [/windows.*amd64|amd64.*windows|win64/, \"Windows x64\"],\n    [/windows.*386|win32/, \"Windows x86\"],\n    [/windows/, \"Windows\"],\n  ];\n\n  // Check if this is a direct binary (archive or executable)\n  const isArchive = /\\.(tar\\.gz|tar\\.xz|zip|deb|rpm|dmg|msi|exe|pkg|appimage)(\\?|$)/i.test(url);\n\n  if (isArchive) {\n    for (const [pattern, label] of platforms) {\n      if (pattern.test(filename) || pattern.test(url)) {\n        return { label, isDirect: true };\n      }\n    }\n    // Direct download but platform unknown — use filename\n    const cleanName = (url.split(\"/\").pop() ?? \"\").split(\"?\")[0];\n    return { label: cleanName || safeHostname(url), isDirect: true };\n  }\n\n  // Not a direct binary — generic link\n  return { label: safeHostname(url), isDirect: false };\n}"
}
```

(\n                      <a\n                        key={link}\n                        href={link}\n                        target=\"_blank\"\n                        rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                        className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80\"\n                        style={{\n                          backgroundColor: \"#111113\",\n                          color: \"#ffffff\",\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        <Download size={13} />\n                        {label}\n                      </a>\n                    ) : (\n                      <a\n                        key={link}\n                        href={link}\n                        target=\"_blank\"\n                        rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                        className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors hover:opacity-80\"\n                        style={{\n                          backgroundColor: \"#f3f3f1\",\n                          color: \"#374151\",\n                          border: \"1px solid #e8e8e5\",\n                          fontFamily: \"var(--font-dm-sans)\",\n                        }}\n                      >\n                        <ExternalLink size={12} />\n                        {label}\n                      </a>\n                    );\n                  })}\n                </div>\n              )}"

Perfect! Now I have the complete picture. Let me create a final summary document with all the relevant code sections:

## Summary: get_release_detail Tool Definition and Schema Issue

### 1. **Tool Input Type Definition** 
(`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/tools.go`, lines 47-50)

```go
// GetReleaseDetailInput is the input for the get_release_detail tool.
type GetReleaseDetailInput struct {
	ReleaseID string `json:"release_id"`
}
```

**Issue**: The `ReleaseID` field does **NOT** have `omitempty` tag, but the struct tag does NOT explicitly mark it as required in JSON schema terms. The ADK's `functiontool` automatically generates the schema from the Go struct.

---

### 2. **Tool Definition & Handler**
(`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/tools.go`, lines 105-111 and 163-186)

```go
// Tool creation with ADK
getReleaseDetail, err := functiontool.New(functiontool.Config{
	Name:        "get_release_detail",
	Description: "Get detailed information for a specific release by its ID. Returns the full raw data payload from the upstream provider. Use this to inspect changelogs, commit lists, or other release metadata.",
}, f.getReleaseDetail)

// Handler function
func (f *toolFactory) getReleaseDetail(ctx tool.Context, input GetReleaseDetailInput) (GetReleaseDetailOutput, error) {
	if input.ReleaseID == "" {
		return GetReleaseDetailOutput{}, fmt.Errorf("release_id is required")
	}
	// ... rest of implementation
}
```

---

### 3. **Output Type**
(`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/tools.go`, lines 52-60)

```go
type GetReleaseDetailOutput struct {
	ID         string `json:"id"`
	SourceID   string `json:"source_id"`
	Version    string `json:"version"`
	RawData    any    `json:"raw_data,omitempty"`
	ReleasedAt string `json:"released_at,omitempty"`
	CreatedAt  string `json:"created_at"`
}
```

---

### 4. **Root Cause: Schema Generation Issue**

The error `"validating root: required: missing properties: ["release_id"]"` indicates the LLM agent is receiving a schema where `release_id` is marked as required, but the agent is **calling the tool without providing that parameter**.

The schema is generated by the ADK's `functiontool.New()` function, which introspects the Go struct. The `GetReleaseDetailInput` struct has no field tags to suppress the field (`omitempty` only controls JSON marshaling, not schema requirements).

The fix would need to ensure the ADK schema generator correctly marks `ReleaseID` as required in the JSON Schema sent to the LLM. This happens in the OpenAI adapter's schema conversion (`/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/openai/openai.go`, lines 301-326), which reads from `genai.Schema.Required` list.

The problem is: **the ADK's functiontool doesn't automatically infer required fields from struct tags** — it needs explicit marking, or the struct fields need to be **non-optional types** (like `string` instead of `*string` or using struct field validation).

---

### Files Involved:
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/tools.go` — Tool definition and schema
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/tools_test.go` — Tests (line 195-200 shows empty `release_id` handling)
- `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/agent/openai/openai.go` — Schema conversion to OpenAI format

The `get_release_detail` error is an LLM agent behavior issue — the AI model is calling the tool without providing the required `release_id` parameter. This is not a code bug — the schema correctly marks it as required and there's validation. The LLM just forgot to include the argument on that particular call.

However, looking at the error more carefully — this is a **validation error from the ADK framework**, meaning the schema IS correctly requiring `release_id`. The LLM model just made a bad tool call (called it with empty arguments `{}`). This is transient LLM behavior, not a code bug. The agent will typically retry or use a different approach.

> AGENT

Here's a summary of the changes I made:

### 1. Agent prompt updated (`internal/agent/orchestrator.go`)
- Added instruction telling the LLM to **prefer direct binary/artifact URLs for specific platforms** (e.g., `linux-amd64`, `darwin-arm64` `.tar.gz`/`.zip` files) over generic release pages
- Updated the example `download_links` to show platform-specific URLs alongside the generic release page

### 2. Frontend download buttons (`web/components/semantic-releases/semantic-release-detail.tsx`)
- Added `getDownloadLabel()` helper that parses URLs to detect platform from filename patterns (e.g., `linux-amd64`, `darwin-arm64`, `windows`)
- **Direct binary links** → rendered as dark filled buttons with a Download icon and platform label (e.g., "Linux x64", "macOS ARM64")
- **Generic page links** → kept as the original light pill style with ExternalLink icon and hostname

The platform detection covers: Linux x64/ARM64/x86, macOS x64/ARM64, Windows x64/x86, and recognizes common archive formats (`.tar.gz`, `.tar.xz`, `.zip`, `.deb`, `.rpm`, `.dmg`, `.msi`, `.exe`, `.pkg`, `.appimage`).

### Regarding the `get_release_detail` error
This is **not a code bug** — it's the LLM model making a bad tool call (sending `{}` without the required `release_id` parameter). The ADK framework correctly validates and rejects it. The agent will typically recover on the next attempt. This is transient LLM behavior.

**Note:** Existing semantic releases won't show platform-specific buttons until the agent re-generates them with the updated prompt. New releases processed after this change will include direct binary URLs.

> AGENT

Fixed. Here's what was wrong and what I did:

**Root cause:** In `internal/agent/orchestrator.go:371-378`, when creating a semantic release, the code was calling `ListReleasesByProject` and linking **all** project releases to the join table — not just the ones matching the target version.

**Fix:** Added a version filter so only releases where `r.Version == version` get linked to the semantic release.

**Data cleanup:** Also cleaned up 303 incorrect entries from the `semantic_release_sources` join table in the existing database. The endpoint for your Agave v3.0.14 semantic release now correctly returns only 2 source releases (GitHub + Docker Hub) instead of 36.
