Excellent. Now let me create a comprehensive summary document with all the notification channel formats. Based on my thorough exploration, I now have the complete picture.

---

## Notification Channel Formats - Complete Analysis

I've conducted a thorough exploration of all notification channel implementations in the Changelogue codebase. Here's the complete breakdown:

### Architecture Overview

All notification channels implement the **`Sender` interface** defined in `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go`:

```go
// Notification is the payload sent through all notification channels.
type Notification struct {
	Title   string `json:"title"`
	Body    string `json:"body"`
	Version string `json:"version"`
}

// Sender is the interface that all notification channel implementations must satisfy.
type Sender interface {
	Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error
}
```

The notification is created in the `NotifyWorker` at `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go` (lines 82-86):

```go
msg := Notification{
	Title:   fmt.Sprintf("New release: %s", release.Version),
	Body:    string(release.RawData),
	Version: release.Version,
}
```

---

### 1. **Webhook Sender** - Generic JSON

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/webhook.go`

**Type:** `webhook`

**Configuration:**
```json
{
  "url": "https://example.com/webhook"
}
```

**Payload Format (most basic):**
Sends the raw `Notification` struct as JSON POST:
```json
{
  "title": "New release: v1.14.0",
  "body": "...",
  "version": "v1.14.0"
}
```

**Key Characteristics:**
- No transformation or special formatting
- Direct JSON serialization of the Notification struct
- Single POST request to configured URL
- Content-Type: `application/json`
- Simplest format - receiver must parse Body themselves

---

### 2. **Slack Sender** - Block Kit (Rich Formatting)

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/slack.go`

**Type:** `slack`

**Configuration:**
```json
{
  "webhook_url": "https://hooks.slack.com/services/YOUR/WEBHOOK/URL"
}
```

**Payload Structure:**
```go
type slackPayload struct {
	Blocks []slackBlock `json:"blocks"`
}

type slackBlock struct {
	Type     string      `json:"type"`
	Text     *slackText  `json:"text,omitempty"`
	Fields   []slackText `json:"fields,omitempty"`
	Elements []slackText `json:"elements,omitempty"`
}

type slackText struct {
	Type string `json:"type"`
	Text string `json:"text"`
}
```

**Two Formatting Modes:**

#### A. Simple Fallback (non-SemanticReport messages)
```json
{
  "blocks": [
    {
      "type": "header",
      "text": {
        "type": "plain_text",
        "text": "New release: geth v1.14.0"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "Released on GitHub with security fixes"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

#### B. Rich Semantic Report Formatting
When Body is valid JSON of a `SemanticReport`, builds multiple blocks:

```go
type SemanticReport struct {
	// SRE-focused fields
	Subject          string   `json:"subject"`
	RiskLevel        string   `json:"risk_level"`
	RiskReason       string   `json:"risk_reason"`
	StatusChecks     []string `json:"status_checks"`
	ChangelogeSummary string   `json:"changelog_summary"`
	DownloadCommands []string `json:"download_commands,omitempty"`
	DownloadLinks    []string `json:"download_links,omitempty"`
	
	// Existing fields
	Summary        string `json:"summary,omitempty"`
	Availability   string `json:"availability"`
	Adoption       string `json:"adoption"`
	Urgency        string `json:"urgency"`
	Recommendation string `json:"recommendation"`
}
```

**Block Composition** (`buildSemanticBlocks` function, lines 57-173):
1. **Header block** - Title
2. **Subject section** - If present, in bold markdown
3. **Risk/Urgency/Availability field section**:
   ```
   Risk Level: 🔴 HIGH
   Urgency: High
   Availability: GA
   ```
   Risk emoji mapping:
   - LOW → 🟢
   - MEDIUM → 🟡
   - HIGH → 🔴
   - CRITICAL → ⚫
   - Default → ⚪

4. **Divider + Risk Assessment section** - If RiskReason present
5. **Divider + Status Checks section** - If present (bulleted list)
6. **Divider + Changelog section** - Uses ChangelogSummary or Summary
7. **Divider + Recommendation section** - If present
8. **Adoption section** - If present
9. **Divider + Download Commands** - Code block with backticks
10. **Download Links section** - If present (angle-bracket links)
11. **Divider + Context footer** - "📡 _Sent by Changelogue_"

**Example Rich Payload:**
```json
{
  "blocks": [
    {
      "type": "header",
      "text": {
        "type": "plain_text",
        "text": "Semantic Release Report: go-ethereum v1.16.4"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "*Ready to Deploy: go-ethereum v1.16.4*"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "fields": [
        {
          "type": "mrkdwn",
          "text": "*Risk Level:*\n🔴 HIGH"
        },
        {
          "type": "mrkdwn",
          "text": "*Urgency:*\nHigh"
        },
        {
          "type": "mrkdwn",
          "text": "*Availability:*\nGA"
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "type": "divider"
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "*Risk Assessment*\nVersion not found in sources"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "divider"
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "*Status Checks*\n• Binaries Unverified\n• Docker Image Unverified"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "*Download Commands*\n```docker pull ethereum/client-go:v1.16.4```"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "section",
      "text": {
        "type": "mrkdwn",
        "text": "*Download Links*\n• <https://github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/releases/tag/v1.16.4>"
      }
    },
    {
      "type": "divider"
    },
    {
      "type": "context",
      "elements": [
        {
          "type": "mrkdwn",
          "text": "📡 _Sent by Changelogue_"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

---

### 3. **Discord Sender** - Embeds

**File:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/discord.go`

**Type:** `discord`

**Configuration:**
```json
{
  "webhook_url": "https://discordapp.com/api/webhooks/YOUR/WEBHOOK"
}
```

**Payload Structure:**
```go
type discordPayload struct {
	Embeds []discordEmbed `json:"embeds"`
}

type discordEmbed struct {
	Title       string              `json:"title"`
	Description string              `json:"description"`
	Color       int                 `json:"color"`
	Fields      []discordEmbedField `json:"fields,omitempty"`
}

type discordEmbedField struct {
	Name   string `json:"name"`
	Value  string `json:"value"`
	Inline bool   `json:"inline"`
}
```

**Payload Format:**
```json
{
  "embeds": [
    {
      "title": "New release: geth v1.14.0",
      "description": "Released on GitHub with security fixes",
      "color": 5793522,
      "fields": [
        {
          "name": "Version",
          "value": "v1.14.0",
          "inline": true
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

**Key Characteristics:**
- Single embed per notification
- Color: `0x5865F2` (Discord blurple - hex 5793522 in decimal)
- Body text truncated to 4096 characters max (Discord limit) with "..." suffix if truncated
- Always includes Version field (inline)
- No special handling for SemanticReport - treats Body as plain text
- Much simpler than Slack (no rich semantic parsing)

---

### Data Flow Comparison

| Field | Webhook | Slack (Simple) | Slack (Semantic) | Discord |
|-------|---------|----------------|------------------|---------|
| **Title** | JSON title | Header block | Header block | Embed title |
| **Body** | JSON body | Mrkdwn section | Multiple blocks (risk, changelog, etc.) | Embed description (truncated to 4096) |
| **Version** | JSON version | Not displayed | Not displayed | Field (inline) |
| **Rich Fields** | None | None | Yes (risk emoji, fields section) | None |
| **Formatting** | None | Markdown blocks | Semantic blocks + dividers | Discord embeds |
| **Semantic Parsing** | No | No | Yes (JSON.Unmarshal check) | No |

---

### Message Creation in NotifyWorker

At `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go` (lines 58-96), the workflow is:

1. Fetch the Release from database
2. List all subscriptions for that source
3. For each subscription:
   - Fetch the NotificationChannel
   - Create a Notification with:
     - `Title`: `"New release: {version}"`
     - `Body`: `release.RawData` (raw JSON string)
     - `Version`: `release.Version`
   - Send via appropriate Sender based on `ch.Type`

---

### Key File Paths

- **Sender Interface:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/sender.go`
- **Webhook Implementation:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/webhook.go`
- **Slack Implementation:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/slack.go`
- **Discord Implementation:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/discord.go`
- **Notification Worker:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/worker.go`
- **Models (SemanticReport):** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/models/semantic_release.go`
- **Tests:** `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/*_test.go`

     1→package routing
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→
    10→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// discordEmbedDescriptionLimit is Discord's maximum embed description length.
    14→const discordEmbedDescriptionLimit = 4096
    15→
    16→type discordConfig struct {
    17→	WebhookURL string `json:"webhook_url"`
    18→}
    19→
    20→// DiscordSender sends notifications as Discord webhook messages with embeds.
    21→type DiscordSender struct {
    22→	Client *http.Client
    23→}
    24→
    25→// discordPayload is the Discord webhook payload.
    26→type discordPayload struct {
    27→	Embeds []discordEmbed `json:"embeds"`
    28→}
    29→
    30→type discordEmbed struct {
    31→	Title       string              `json:"title"`
    32→	Description string              `json:"description"`
    33→	Color       int                 `json:"color"`
    34→	Fields      []discordEmbedField `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    35→}
    36→
    37→type discordEmbedField struct {
    38→	Name   string `json:"name"`
    39→	Value  string `json:"value"`
    40→	Inline bool   `json:"inline"`
    41→}
    42→
    43→func (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
    44→	var cfg discordConfig
    45→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
    46→		return fmt.Errorf("parse discord config: %w", err)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	description := msg.Body
    50→	if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
    51→		description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	payload := discordPayload{
    55→		Embeds: []discordEmbed{
    56→			{
    57→				Title:       msg.Title,
    58→				Description: description,
    59→				Color:       0x5865F2, // Discord blurple
    60→				Fields: []discordEmbedField{
    61→					{Name: "Version", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},
    62→				},
    63→			},
    64→		},
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal discord payload: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    77→
    78→	client := s.Client
    79→	if client == nil {
    80→		client = http.DefaultClient
    81→	}
    82→
    83→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		return fmt.Errorf("send discord notification: %w", err)
    86→	}
    87→	defer resp.Body.Close()
    88→
    89→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
    90→		return fmt.Errorf("discord returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
    91→	}
    92→	return nil
    93→}
    94→

     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models"
    12→)
    13→
    14→type slackConfig struct {
    15→	WebhookURL string `json:"webhook_url"`
    16→}
    17→
    18→// SlackSender sends notifications as Slack Block Kit messages via incoming webhook.
    19→type SlackSender struct {
    20→	Client *http.Client
    21→}
    22→
    23→// slackPayload is the Slack incoming webhook payload using Block Kit.
    24→type slackPayload struct {
    25→	Blocks []slackBlock `json:"blocks"`
    26→}
    27→
    28→type slackBlock struct {
    29→	Type     string      `json:"type"`
    30→	Text     *slackText  `json:"text,omitempty"`
    31→	Fields   []slackText `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    32→	Elements []slackText `json:"elements,omitempty"`
    33→}
    34→
    35→type slackText struct {
    36→	Type string `json:"type"`
    37→	Text string `json:"text"`
    38→}
    39→
    40→// riskEmoji returns an emoji indicator for the given risk level.
    41→func riskEmoji(level string) string {
    42→	switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {
    43→	case "LOW":
    44→		return "🟢"
    45→	case "MEDIUM":
    46→		return "🟡"
    47→	case "HIGH":
    48→		return "🔴"
    49→	case "CRITICAL":
    50→		return "⚫"
    51→	default:
    52→		return "⚪"
    53→	}
    54→}
    55→
    56→// buildSemanticBlocks builds rich Slack Block Kit blocks from a SemanticReport.
    57→func buildSemanticBlocks(title string, report *models.SemanticReport) []slackBlock {
    58→	blocks := []slackBlock{
    59→		{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: title}},
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Subject as the lead section
    63→	if report.Subject != "" {
    64→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    65→			Type: "section",
    66→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*%s*", report.Subject)},
    67→		})
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	// Risk + Urgency + Availability as compact fields
    71→	blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
    72→		Type: "section",
    73→		Fields: []slackText{
    74→			{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Risk Level:*\n%s %s", riskEmoji(report.RiskLevel), report.RiskLevel)},
    75→			{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Urgency:*\n%s", report.Urgency)},
    76→			{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Availability:*\n%s", report.Availability)},
    77→		},
    78→	})
    79→
    80→	// Risk reason
    81→	if report.RiskReason != "" {
    82→		blocks = append(blocks,
    83→			slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
    84→			slackBlock{
    85→				Type: "section",
    86→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Risk Assessment*\n%s", report.RiskReason)},
    87→			},
    88→		)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Status checks
    92→	if len(report.StatusChecks) > 0 {
    93→		var items []string
    94→		for _, check := range report.StatusChecks {
    95→			items = append(items, fmt.Sprintf("• %s", check))
    96→		}
    97→		blocks = append(blocks,
    98→			slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
    99→			slackBlock{
   100→				Type: "section",
   101→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Status Checks*\n%s", strings.Join(items, "\n"))},
   102→			},
   103→		)
   104→	}
   105→
   106→	// Changelog summary
   107→	summary := report.ChangelogSummary
   108→	if summary == "" {
   109→		summary = report.Summary
   110→	}
   111→	if summary != "" {
   112→		blocks = append(blocks,
   113→			slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
   114→			slackBlock{
   115→				Type: "section",
   116→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Changelog*\n%s", summary)},
   117→			},
   118→		)
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// Recommendation + Adoption
   122→	if report.Recommendation != "" {
   123→		blocks = append(blocks,
   124→			slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
   125→			slackBlock{
   126→				Type: "section",
   127→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Recommendation*\n%s", report.Recommendation)},
   128→			},
   129→		)
   130→	}
   131→	if report.Adoption != "" {
   132→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   133→			Type: "section",
   134→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Adoption*\n%s", report.Adoption)},
   135→		})
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Download commands as code block
   139→	if len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {
   140→		blocks = append(blocks,
   141→			slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
   142→			slackBlock{
   143→				Type: "section",
   144→				Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Download Commands*\n```%s```", strings.Join(report.DownloadCommands, "\n"))},
   145→			},
   146→		)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Download links
   150→	if len(report.DownloadLinks) > 0 {
   151→		var links []string
   152→		for _, link := range report.DownloadLinks {
   153→			links = append(links, fmt.Sprintf("• <%s>", link))
   154→		}
   155→		blocks = append(blocks, slackBlock{
   156→			Type: "section",
   157→			Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: fmt.Sprintf("*Download Links*\n%s", strings.Join(links, "\n"))},
   158→		})
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	// Footer context
   162→	blocks = append(blocks,
   163→		slackBlock{Type: "divider"},
   164→		slackBlock{
   165→			Type: "context",
   166→			Elements: []slackText{
   167→				{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: "📡 _Sent by Changelogue_"},
   168→			},
   169→		},
   170→	)
   171→
   172→	return blocks
   173→}
   174→
   175→func (s *SlackSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
   176→	var cfg slackConfig
   177→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
   178→		return fmt.Errorf("parse slack config: %w", err)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Try to parse body as a SemanticReport for rich formatting.
   182→	var blocks []slackBlock
   183→	var report models.SemanticReport
   184→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != "" {
   185→		blocks = buildSemanticBlocks(msg.Title, &report)
   186→	} else {
   187→		// Fallback: simple header + body for non-report messages.
   188→		blocks = []slackBlock{
   189→			{Type: "header", Text: &slackText{Type: "plain_text", Text: msg.Title}},
   190→			{Type: "section", Text: &slackText{Type: "mrkdwn", Text: msg.Body}},
   191→		}
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	payload := slackPayload{Blocks: blocks}
   195→
   196→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
   197→	if err != nil {
   198→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal slack payload: %w", err)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))
   202→	if err != nil {
   203→		return fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
   204→	}
   205→	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   206→
   207→	client := s.Client
   208→	if client == nil {
   209→		client = http.DefaultClient
   210→	}
   211→
   212→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return fmt.Errorf("send slack notification: %w", err)
   215→	}
   216→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   217→
   218→	if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
   219→		return fmt.Errorf("slack returned status %d", resp.StatusCode)
   220→	}
   221→	return nil
   222→}
   223→

     1→# Lessons Learned
     2→
     3→## 2026-02-25
     4→
     5→### Implementation plans must include doc updates inline
     6→
     7→**Pattern:** When writing implementation plans, always include updates to existing documentation (ARCH.md, DESIGN.md, API design docs) as part of each relevant task — not as a standalone final task. Each task should update the docs that correspond to the code it changes.
     8→
     9→**Why:** Deferring doc updates to the end means they get forgotten or become a massive catch-up task. Inline doc updates keep documentation in sync with code changes and make each commit self-contained.
    10→
    11→**Rule:** For every task that changes architecture, models, API endpoints, or component design:
    12→- Include the relevant doc files in the task's "Files:" list
    13→- Add a step to update the corresponding doc section
    14→- Include the doc files in the commit
    15→

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/docs/plans/lessons.md",
  "old_string": "- Include the doc files in the commit",
  "new_string": "- Include the doc files in the commit\n\n### Notification format must be aligned across all channels\n\n**Pattern:** When adding or changing information displayed in one channel (Slack, Discord, webhook, web UI), check ALL other channels and update them to show the same data fields. Never add rich formatting to one channel without updating the others.\n\n**Why:** Slack had full SemanticReport parsing (risk, urgency, status checks, download links, etc.) while Discord just dumped raw JSON as description text. Users on different channels got wildly different notification quality. The web UI also had its own representation that diverged from both.\n\n**Rule:** All notification representations (Slack, Discord, webhook, web UI) must show the same semantic report fields:\n- Subject, Risk Level + Reason, Urgency, Status Checks, Changelog Summary, Adoption, Download Commands, Download Links\n- Each channel should use its native formatting (Block Kit for Slack, Embeds for Discord, structured JSON for webhook)\n- When adding a new field to the SemanticReport model, update ALL senders and the web detail component\n- Test notifications across all configured channels, not just one"
}

    13→// discordEmbedDescriptionLimit is Discord's maximum embed description length.
    14→const discordEmbedDescriptionLimit = 4096
    15→
    16→type discordConfig struct {
    17→	WebhookURL string `json:"webhook_url"`
    18→}
    19→
    20→// DiscordSender sends notifications as Discord webhook messages with embeds.

    23→}
    24→
    25→// discordPayload is the Discord webhook payload.
    26→type discordPayload struct {
    27→	Embeds []discordEmbed `json:"embeds"`
    28→}
    29→
    30→type discordEmbed struct {
    31→	Title       string              `json:"title"`
    32→	Description string              `json:"description"`
    33→	Color       int                 `json:"color"`
    34→	Fields      []discordEmbedField `json:"fields,omitempty"`
    35→}
    36→
    37→type discordEmbedField struct {
    38→	Name   string `json:"name"`
    39→	Value  string `json:"value"`
    40→	Inline bool   `json:"inline"`
    41→}
    42→
    43→func (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {
    44→	var cfg discordConfig
    45→	if err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {
    46→		return fmt.Errorf("parse discord config: %w", err)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	description := msg.Body
    50→	if len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {
    51→		description = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + "..."
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	payload := discordPayload{
    55→		Embeds: []discordEmbed{
    56→			{
    57→				Title:       msg.Title,
    58→				Description: description,
    59→				Color:       0x5865F2, // Discord blurple
    60→				Fields: []discordEmbedField{
    61→					{Name: "Version", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},
    62→				},
    63→			},
    64→		},
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	body, err := json.Marshal(payload)
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("marshal discord payload: %w", err)
    70→	}

discordEmbedDescriptionLimit is Discord's maximum embed description length.\nconst discordEmbedDescriptionLimit = 4096\n\ntype discordConfig struct {\n\tWebhookURL string `json:\"webhook_url\"`\n}\n\n// DiscordSender sends notifications as Discord webhook messages with embeds.\ntype DiscordSender struct {\n\tClient *http.Client\n}\n\n// discordPayload is the Discord webhook payload.\ntype discordPayload struct {\n\tEmbeds []discordEmbed `json:\"embeds\"`\n}\n\ntype discordEmbed struct {\n\tTitle       string              `json:\"title\"`\n\tDescription string              `json:\"description\"`\n\tColor       int                 `json:\"color\"`\n\tFields      []discordEmbedField `json:\"fields,omitempty\"`\n}\n\ntype discordEmbedField struct {\n\tName   string `json:\"name\"`\n\tValue  string `json:\"value\"`\n\tInline bool   `json:\"inline\"`\n}\n\n// discordRiskColor returns a Discord embed color based on risk level.\nfunc discordRiskColor(level string) int {\n\tswitch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(level)) {\n\tcase \"CRITICAL\":\n\t\treturn 0x111113 // near-black\n\tcase \"HIGH\":\n\t\treturn 0xDC2626 // red\n\tcase \"MEDIUM\":\n\t\treturn 0xD97706 // amber\n\tcase \"LOW\":\n\t\treturn 0x16A34A // green\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn 0x5865F2 // Discord blurple\n\t}\n}\n\n// buildSemanticEmbed builds a rich Discord embed from a SemanticReport.\nfunc buildSemanticEmbed(title string, version string, report *models.SemanticReport) discordEmbed {\n\tvar descParts []string\n\n\tif report.Subject != \"\" {\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf(\"**%s**\", report.Subject))\n\t}\n\n\tif report.RiskReason != \"\" {\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf(\">>> %s\", report.RiskReason))\n\t}\n\n\tif len(report.StatusChecks) > 0 {\n\t\tvar checks []string\n\t\tfor _, check := range report.StatusChecks {\n\t\t\tchecks = append(checks, fmt.Sprintf(\"✅ %s\", check))\n\t\t}\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, strings.Join(checks, \"\\n\"))\n\t}\n\n\tsummary := report.ChangelogSummary\n\tif summary == \"\" {\n\t\tsummary = report.Summary\n\t}\n\tif summary != \"\" {\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf(\"**Changelog**\\n%s\", summary))\n\t}\n\n\tif len(report.DownloadCommands) > 0 {\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf(\"**Download Commands**\\n```\\n%s\\n```\", strings.Join(report.DownloadCommands, \"\\n\")))\n\t}\n\n\tif len(report.DownloadLinks) > 0 {\n\t\tvar links []string\n\t\tfor _, link := range report.DownloadLinks {\n\t\t\tlinks = append(links, fmt.Sprintf(\"• %s\", link))\n\t\t}\n\t\tdescParts = append(descParts, fmt.Sprintf(\"**Download Links**\\n%s\", strings.Join(links, \"\\n\")))\n\t}\n\n\tdescription := strings.Join(descParts, \"\\n\\n\")\n\tif len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {\n\t\tdescription = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + \"...\"\n\t}\n\n\tfields := []discordEmbedField{\n\t\t{Name: \"Risk Level\", Value: fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %s\", riskEmoji(report.RiskLevel), report.RiskLevel), Inline: true},\n\t\t{Name: \"Urgency\", Value: report.Urgency, Inline: true},\n\t}\n\tif report.Availability != \"\" {\n\t\tfields = append(fields, discordEmbedField{Name: \"Availability\", Value: report.Availability, Inline: true})\n\t}\n\tif report.Adoption != \"\" {\n\t\tfields = append(fields, discordEmbedField{Name: \"Adoption\", Value: report.Adoption, Inline: false})\n\t}\n\n\treturn discordEmbed{\n\t\tTitle:       title,\n\t\tDescription: description,\n\t\tColor:       discordRiskColor(report.RiskLevel),\n\t\tFields:      fields,\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (s *DiscordSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {\n\tvar cfg discordConfig\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse discord config: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar embed discordEmbed\n\tvar report models.SemanticReport\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != \"\" {\n\t\tembed = buildSemanticEmbed(msg.Title, msg.Version, &report)\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Fallback: simple embed for non-report messages.\n\t\tdescription := msg.Body\n\t\tif len(description) > discordEmbedDescriptionLimit {\n\t\t\tdescription = description[:discordEmbedDescriptionLimit-3] + \"...\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tembed = discordEmbed{\n\t\t\tTitle:       msg.Title,\n\t\t\tDescription: description,\n\t\t\tColor:       0x5865F2,\n\t\t\tFields: []discordEmbedField{\n\t\t\t\t{Name: \"Version\", Value: msg.Version, Inline: true},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpayload := discordPayload{Embeds: []discordEmbed{embed}}\n\n\tbody, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal discord payload: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.WebhookURL, bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\n\tclient := s.Client\n\tif client == nil {\n\t\tclient = http.DefaultClient\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"send discord notification: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode >= 400 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"discord returned status %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/routing/webhook.go",
  "content": "package routing\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\n\t\"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/models\"\n)\n\ntype webhookConfig struct {\n\tURL string `json:\"url\"`\n}\n\n// WebhookSender sends notifications as JSON POST requests to a configured URL.\ntype WebhookSender struct {\n\tClient *http.Client\n}\n\n// webhookSemanticPayload is the structured webhook payload for semantic reports.\ntype webhookSemanticPayload struct {\n\tTitle   string                `json:\"title\"`\n\tVersion string                `json:\"version\"`\n\tReport  *models.SemanticReport `json:\"report\"`\n}\n\nfunc (s *WebhookSender) Send(ctx context.Context, ch *models.NotificationChannel, msg Notification) error {\n\tvar cfg webhookConfig\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(ch.Config, &cfg); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"parse webhook config: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Try to parse body as SemanticReport for structured output.\n\tvar payload any\n\tvar report models.SemanticReport\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(msg.Body), &report); err == nil && report.Subject != \"\" {\n\t\tpayload = webhookSemanticPayload{\n\t\t\tTitle:   msg.Title,\n\t\t\tVersion: msg.Version,\n\t\t\tReport:  &report,\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tpayload = msg\n\t}\n\n\tbody, err := json.Marshal(payload)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"marshal notification: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, cfg.URL, bytes.NewReader(body))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"create request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\n\tclient := s.Client\n\tif client == nil {\n\t\tclient = http.DefaultClient\n\t}\n\n\tresp, err := client.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"send webhook: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode >= 400 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"webhook returned status %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Here's what I did:

**Discord** (`discord.go`) — Complete rewrite to parse `SemanticReport` JSON, same as Slack:
- Risk-colored embed (red for HIGH, amber for MEDIUM, green for LOW, black for CRITICAL)
- Subject as bold header, risk reason as blockquote
- Status checks with checkmark emoji
- Changelog summary, download commands (code block), download links
- Fields for Risk Level (with emoji), Urgency, Availability, Adoption
- Fallback to simple embed for non-report messages

**Webhook** (`webhook.go`) — Now sends structured `{ title, version, report: { ... } }` with the full `SemanticReport` object instead of raw JSON body string

**Slack** (`slack.go`) — Removed the "Recommendation" section to align with web UI

**Web UI** — Removed recommendation blockquote (per your earlier request)

**Lessons** (`docs/plans/lessons.md`) — Added rule: "When adding/changing info in one channel, update ALL channels to show the same fields"
